Administration (NHTSA) of a safety recall which will be conducted pursuant to Federal law for the
product(s) listed below. Please review the following information to ensure that it conforms to your
records as this information is being made available to the public. If the information does not agree with
your records, please contact us immediately to discuss your concerns.

Makes/Models/Model Years:
TESLA/MODEL 3/2024
TESLA/MODEL Y/2026

Mfr's Report Date: July 07, 2026
NHTSA Campaign Number: 26V445
Components:
Potential Number of Units Affected: 15
Problem Description:
Tesla, Inc. (Tesla) is recalling certain 2026 Model Y and 2024 Model 3 vehicles. The vehicles were
originally built for a foreign market and do not have knee air bags, may not be equipped with certain
interior safety countermeasures, and may not have been tested for bumper impact resistance
procedures. As such, these vehicles fail to comply with the requirements of Federal Motor Vehicle
Safety Standard numbers, 208, "Occupant Crash Protection," 201, "Occupant Protection in Interior
Impact," and Part 581, "Bumper Standards."

Consequence:
A noncompliant vehicle may have reduced occupant protections, increasing the risk of injury during a
crash.

Remedy:
Tesla will repurchase noncompliant vehicles currently registered in the United States, free of charge.
Owner notification letters are expected to be mailed September 5, 2026. Owners may contact Tesla
customer service at 1-877-798-3752 or resolutions@tesla.com. Tesla's number for this recall is SB-26-
00-004.

Notes:
Owners may also contact the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration Vehicle Safety Hotline at
1-888-327-4236 (TTY 1-888-275-9171), or go to www.nhtsa.gov.

Please ensure the following requirements are met:

 Involved Component Not Supplied
 Provide the part number, name and description of the component(s) involved in this recall as
 required by 49 USC § 30119 in an amended 573 in the "Involved Components" section through the
 NHTSA Recalls Portal.
 AMENDED 573 REQUIRED.

Please be reminded of the following requirements:
You are required to submit a draft owner notification letter to this office no less than five days prior to
mailing it to the customers. Also, copies of all notices, bulletins, dealer notifications, and other
communications that relate to this recall, including a copy of the final owner notification letter and any
subsequent owner follow-up notification letter(s), are required to be submitted to this office no later than
5 days after they are originally sent (if they are sent to more than one manufacturer, distributor, dealer,
or purchaser/owner).

Under 49 U.S.C. § 30112(a), it is illegal for anyone, including a manufacturer, distributor, dealer, or
retailer to sell an item of equipment or vehicle that fails to comply with all applicable Federal motor
vehicle safety standards.

As stated in 49 U.S.C. § 30118(f), submission of eight consecutive quarterly reports followed by three
annual reports is required. As described in 573.7, submission of the first of eight consecutive quarterly
status reports is required within one month after the close of the calendar quarter in which notification to
purchasers occurs. Therefore, the first quarterly report will be due on, or before, 30 days after the close
of the calendar quarter. The first of three consecutive annual status reports will be due on, or before, 1
year after the eighth quarterly report was submitted.

Tesla, Inc.'s contact for this recall will be Sarah Shiver who may be reached by email at
sarah.shiver@dot.gov. We look forward to working with you.


Sincerely,



Alex Ansley
Chief, Recall Management Division
Office of Defects Investigation
Enforcement

Definitions:
Report Quarter: The quarter the manufacturer is reporting recall completion figures (e.g. 2012-3 means the 3rd quarter of 2012).
Recall Population: The total number of products recalled by the manufacturer.
Total Remedied: The total number of products either remedied, inspected without needing remedy, or returned to inventory.
Total Unreachable: Products deemed unreachable as owner notifications were unable to be delivered.
Total Removed: Products that have been scrapped, stolen, or exported.

                              Manufacturer Name: Tesla, Inc.

Traffic Safety
Administration             Manufacturer Recall No.: SB-26-00-004
Manufacturer Information                                    Population

Manufacturer Name: Tesla, Inc.                              Total number of potentially involved: 15
          Address: 1 Tesla Road                               Estimated percentage with defect: 100%
                         Austin TX, 78725


Vehicle Information

                     Vehicle 1: 2024-2024 TESLA MODEL 3
           Product Category: Light Vehicles
                Product Type:
            Fuel / Propulsion:
            Production Dates: Dec 21, 2023 - Jul 30, 2024
Number of potentially involved: 12
     Descriptive Information:
The recall population includes certain Model Year (“MY”) 2024 Tesla Model 3 vehicles manufactured
between December 21, 2023, and July 30, 2024, and certain MY 2026 Tesla Model Y vehicles
manufactured between August 17, 2025, and February 16, 2026. The affected vehicles were
manufactured at Tesla's Shanghai factory or Tesla's Berlin factory to comply with Canadian Motor
Vehicle Safety Standards (CMVSS) and may have been subsequently imported by customers into the
U.S. from Canada.




                     Vehicle 2: 2026-2026 TESLA MODEL Y
           Product Category: Light Vehicles
                Product Type:
            Fuel / Propulsion:
            Production Dates: Aug 17, 2025 - Feb 16, 2026
Number of potentially involved: 3
     Descriptive Information:
The recall population includes certain Model Year (“MY”) 2024 Tesla Model 3 vehicles manufactured
between December 21, 2023, and July 30, 2024, and certain MY 2026 Tesla Model Y vehicles
manufactured between August 17, 2025, and February 16, 2026. The affected vehicles were
manufactured at Tesla's Shanghai factory or Tesla's Berlin factory to comply with Canadian Motor


                    The information contained in this report was submitted pursuant to 49 CFR § 573
                                                                                                       Page 2 of 5


Part 573 Safety Recall Report                                                                        26V445
Vehicle Safety Standards (CMVSS) and may have been subsequently imported by customers into the
U.S. from Canada.




Defect / Noncompliance Description

Description of the defect or noncompliance:
Because the affected vehicles were not originally manufactured to comply with FMVSS, they are not
equipped with a knee airbag and therefore, do not meet the unbelted test requirements in FMVSS No.
208, S5.1.2(b) and S15.2. In addition, affected Tesla Model Y vehicles are not equipped with
countermeasures required to meet FMVSS No. 201, S6. Moreover, the affected Model 3 vehicles were
not tested pursuant to the bumper impact resistance procedures required by Part 581.

FMVSS1: 201 - Occupant protection in interior impact
FMVSS2: 208 - Occupant crash protection
Description of the safety risk, including crash, fire, death, injury:
Noncompliance with FMVSS Nos. 208 and 201 may result in reduced occupant protection during a
collision, increasing the risk of injury.

Description of the cause:


Identification of any warning that can occur:



Component Manufacturer

          Tier of Supplier:
            Supplier Type:
                    Name:
                  Address:
                  Country:

Involved Components

   Component Name 1: knee airbag
 Component Description:

                   The information contained in this report was submitted pursuant to 49 CFR § 573
                                                                                                     Page 3 of 5


Part 573 Safety Recall Report                                                                      26V445
Component Part Number:

Chronology

     Between December 21, 2023, and February 16, 2026, the affected vehicles were manufactured at
     Tesla's Shanghai factory or Tesla's Berlin factory to comply with all CMVSS and were eventually
     sold to customers in the Canadian market.

     Between June 4, 2024, and April 3, 2026, in response to requests received through its customary
     customer communication channels from customers seeking letters of compliance to satisfy the
     documentation required to import the affected vehicles from Canada into the U.S. under
     Declaration Box 2B, pursuant to 49 C.F.R. 591.5(g), Tesla issued letters of compliance for the
     affected vehicles, confirming that the vehicles complied with all applicable Federal motor vehicle
     safety, bumper, and theft prevention standards, except for the certain standards related to labeling
     and daytime running lamps outlined in 49 C.F.R. 591.5(g).

     On April 28, 2026, upon receiving a letter of compliance request from a customer outside the
     customary communication channel, Tesla initiated an internal review of its importation verification
     procedures for vehicles produced at Tesla’s international factories.

     From April 28 through June 22, 2026, Tesla investigated this issue to understand the design and
     vehicle content differences between vehicles built at the different assembly plants, and whether
     the differences in vehicle content between markets affected compliance with FMVSS
     requirements. On April 28, 2026, Tesla ceased issuing letters of compliance for MY 2024 and later
     Model 3 and Model Y vehicles while Tesla conducted its internal review. On May 8, Tesla refined
     the scope of its investigation and ceased issuing letters of compliance for all vehicles
     manufactured at its Shanghai or Berlin factories while Tesla continued to investigate.

     On May 28, 2026, Tesla met with NHTSA’s Office of the Chief Counsel (OCC) to discuss the
     subject vehicles, and on June 3, 2026, Tesla followed up with OCC with additional information
     regarding the subject vehicles that was requested by OCC during the May 28, 2026, meeting.

     On June 29, 2026, after completing the internal investigation, Tesla determined that the affected
     vehicles are not compliant with FMVSS Nos. 201 and 208, and made a voluntary recall
     determination.

     As of June 30, 2026, Tesla has identified 0 warranty claims and 0 field reports that may be related
     to the condition. Tesla is not aware of any collisions, fatalities, or injuries that are or may be
     related to the condition.



Related NHTSA Recall Number:

Description of Remedy

Remedy Type:
Consumer Advisories:       Do Not Drive        Park Outside


                 The information contained in this report was submitted pursuant to 49 CFR § 573
                                                                                                      Page 4 of 5


Part 573 Safety Recall Report                                                                       26V445
Description of remedy program:
For the affected vehicles that were imported to the United States under 49 CFR Part 591.5(g), and are
currently registered in the United States, Tesla will repurchase the vehicles free of charge in
accordance with 49 U.S. Code § 30120 and as described in the terms set forth in Tesla’s letter to
owners. For all affected vehicles, Tesla will rescind the previously issued letters of compliance and send
customers new letters that correctly describe the vehicles as noncompliant to certain FMVSS.

How remedy component differs from recalled component:
The remedy is to repurchase the noncompliant vehicles that have been imported by customers into the
United States and are currently registered in the United States. Tesla will also rescind the previously
issued letters of compliance for all affected vehicles and send customers new letters that correctly
describe the vehicles as noncompliant to certain FMVSS.

Identify how/when recall condition was corrected in production:
As of May 8, 2026, Tesla ceased issuing letters of compliance for vehicles manufactured at its
Shanghai and Berlin factories that do not meet the FMVSS except for the labeling requirements of
FMVSS Nos. 101 and 110 or 120, and/or the specifications of FMVSS No. 108 relating to daytime
running lamps, as permitted by 49 C.F.R. 591.5(g).



Reimbursement Plan

Description of reimbursement program:
Tesla does not plan to include a statement in the Part 577 owner notification about pre-notice
reimbursement to owners because no vehicle owner incurred costs for a pre-notification remedy
associated with the noncompliance.

Period of reimbursement:
Costs to be reimbursed:
Address for reimbursement claims:


Recall Schedule

Description of recall schedule:
All Tesla stores and service centers will be notified about this recall on or shortly after July 10, 2026.
Owner notification letters will be mailed in accordance with 49 C.F.R. § 577.7.

Planned Dealer Notification Date: Jul 10, 2026 - Jul 10, 2026                        No Dealers

Planned Interim Owner Notification Date:                                             No Owners

Planned Remedy Owner Notification Date: Sep 05, 2026 - Sep 05, 2026                  Phased Recall

                                        IMPORTANT SAFETY RECALL
<Date>

<Name>
<Address>
<Address 2> (if applicable; if not, remove this line)
<City, State, ZIP>

This notice applies to your vehicle, <VIN>

NHTSA Recall No: 26V-445

Dear Tesla Owner,

This notice is sent to you in accordance with the National Traffic and Motor Vehicle Safety Act. Tesla, Inc. has
decided that certain Model Year (“MY”) 2024 Tesla Model 3 and MY 2026 Tesla Model Y vehicles fail to conform to
the Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard numbers 208, "Occupant Crash Protection," 201, "Occupant Protection in
Interior Impact," and Part 581, "Bumper Standards." Our records indicate that you are the owner of a vehicle affected
by this action.


REASON FOR THIS RECALL

Because your vehicle was not originally manufactured to comply with U.S. Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standards
(“FMVSS”), it is not equipped with a knee airbag and therefore, does not meet the unbelted test requirements in
FMVSS No. 208, S5.1.2(b) and S15.2. In addition, affected Tesla Model Y vehicles are not equipped with
countermeasures required to meet FMVSS No. 201, S6. Moreover, the affected Model 3 vehicles were not tested
pursuant to the bumper impact resistance procedures required by Part 581. A noncompliant vehicle may have
reduced occupant protections, increasing the risk of injury during a crash.

WHAT TESLA WILL DO

If you imported your vehicle to the United States under 49 CFR Part 591.5(g), and your vehicle is currently
registered in the United States, Tesla will repurchase your vehicle in accordance with 49 U.S. Code § 30120 and as
described in the terms set forth in this notification. Enclosed with this notification is a letter that rescinds the
Confirmation of Compliance you previously received and correctly describes your vehicle as noncompliant to certain
FMVSS.

WHAT YOU SHOULD DO

Please contact us by email at resolutions@tesla.com or call 1-877-79-TESLA (1-877-798-3752) to initiate the vehicle
repurchase process if your vehicle has been imported and registered in the U.S. If you have not yet imported and
registered your vehicle in the U.S., the Tesla-issued Confirmation of Compliance letter that you previously received
cannot be used to support importation under 49 CFR Part 591.5(g). Please see the enclosed letter from Tesla.

If you have already completed the vehicle repurchase process, no further action is required. To track the status of
this recall for your vehicle, you may use our online VIN look-up tool at https://www.Tesla.com/Vin-Recall-Search.

Federal law requires any lessor who receives a notification of a determination of a noncompliance pertaining to any
leased motor vehicle to send the notice to the lessee within 10 days.

If your dealer fails or is unable to remedy this defect without charge, or within a reasonable amount of time, you may
submit a written complaint to:
         Administrator
         National Highway Traffic Safety Administration
         1200 New Jersey AVE SE
         Washington, DC 20590
You may also call the toll-free Vehicle Safety Hotline at 1-888-327-4236 (TTY: 1-800-424-9153); or go to
http://www.safercar.gov.
If you no longer own the vehicle, update vehicle ownership in your Tesla Account. For more details on adding or
removing vehicles from your Tesla Account, visit www.tesla.com/support/account-support#add-remove-products.

Thank you for being a Tesla customer. We are committed to ensuring that your vehicle provides the
highest possible level of safety.

TESLA, INC.

Condition
Certain Model 3 and Model Y vehicles manufactured for the Canadian market might have been imported by customers
into the United States. These vehicles are noncompliant with United States Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standards
(FMVSS) 201 and FMVSS 208.

Subject: Low Beam Headlights May Be Too Bright/FMVSS 108
Dear Ganga Loganathan:

This letter serves to acknowledge Tesla, Inc.'s notification to the National Highway Traffic Safety
Administration (NHTSA) of a safety recall which will be conducted pursuant to Federal law for the
product(s) listed below. Please review the following information to ensure that it conforms to your
records as this information is being made available to the public. If the information does not agree with
your records, please contact us immediately to discuss your concerns.

Makes/Models/Model Years:
TESLA/MODEL 3/2017-2023
TESLA/MODEL Y/2020-2023

Mfr's Report Date: August 04, 2026
NHTSA Campaign Number: 26V507
Components:
EXTERIOR LIGHTING:HEADLIGHTS

Potential Number of Units Affected: 20,349
Problem Description:
Tesla, Inc. (Tesla) is recalling certain 2017-2023 Model 3 and 2020-2023 Model Y vehicles. The
headlight low beams may be to be too bright and exceed the maximum light output. As such, these
vehicles fail to comply with the requirements of Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard (FMVSS)
number 108, "Lamps, Reflective Devices, and Associated Equipment."

Consequence:
Headlight low beams that are too bright can reduce visibility for oncoming drivers, increasing the risk of
a crash.

Remedy:
The remedy is currently under development. Owner notification letters are expected to be mailed
September 15, 2026. Owners may contact Tesla customer service at 1-877-798-3752. Tesla's number
for this recall is SB-24-17-003.

Notes:
Owners may also contact the National Highway Traffic Safety Administration Vehicle Safety Hotline at
1-888-327-4236 (TTY 1-888-275-9171), or go to www.nhtsa.gov.

Please ensure the following requirements are met:

 Remedy Not Supplied
 Provide a description of the recall remedy as required by 49 CFR §573.6(8)(i), in an amended Part
 573 Recall Report through the NHTSA Recalls Portal.

 AMENDED 573 REQUIRED.

Please be reminded of the following requirements:
You are required to submit a draft owner notification letter to this office no less than five days prior to
mailing it to the customers. Also, copies of all notices, bulletins, dealer notifications, and other
communications that relate to this recall, including a copy of the final owner notification letter and any
subsequent owner follow-up notification letter(s), are required to be submitted to this office no later than
5 days after they are originally sent (if they are sent to more than one manufacturer, distributor, dealer,
or purchaser/owner).

Under 49 U.S.C. § 30112(a), it is illegal for anyone, including a manufacturer, distributor, dealer, or
retailer to sell an item of equipment or vehicle that fails to comply with all applicable Federal motor
vehicle safety standards.

As stated in 49 U.S.C. § 30118(f), submission of eight consecutive quarterly reports followed by three
annual reports is required. As described in 573.7, submission of the first of eight consecutive quarterly
status reports is required within one month after the close of the calendar quarter in which notification to
purchasers occurs. Therefore, the first quarterly report will be due on, or before, 30 days after the close
of the calendar quarter. The first of three consecutive annual status reports will be due on, or before, 1
year after the eighth quarterly report was submitted.

Tesla, Inc.'s contact for this recall will be Sarah Shiver who may be reached by email at
sarah.shiver@dot.gov. We look forward to working with you.


Sincerely,



Alex Ansley
Chief, Recall Management Division
Office of Defects Investigation

===== NHTSA/Recalls/26V507000/RCHRN-26V507-7003.pdf =====
Chronology of the Noncompliance Determination:

   •   On September 6, 2023, an independent test laboratory contracted with Transport
       Canada informed Tesla that the laboratory would be conducting CMVSS No. 108
       testing on a MY 2019 Tesla Model 3 left-hand headlamp (1077371-98-L) that was
       manufactured on June 2, 2023, as part of Transport Canada’s annual compliance
       program. Tesla later provided requested information to Transport Canada, including
       information to facilitate communication between the supplier and the test
       laboratory, given that the testing would be performed at the component-level.

   •   On October 13, 2023, Tesla was informed that the testing was successfully
       completed.

   •   On January 16, 2024, Transport Canada informed Tesla that, based on the testing,
       the Model 3 left-hand headlamp did not comply with the low beam photometric
       requirement of CMVSS No. 108 and exceeded the maximum intensity allowed in the
       10U to 90U zone.

   •   Beginning on January 22, 2024, Tesla commenced a compliance evaluation of the
       headlamp against CMVSS and FMVSS Nos. 108. Through March 1, 2024, Tesla
       worked with the supplier to analyze the test report and compile headlamp
       production data. Tesla also performed additional headlamp tests for evaluation.

   •   From March 3, 2024, through March 7, 2024, Tesla reviewed the completed analysis
       and additional testing.

   •   On March 9, 2024, having completed its compliance evaluation, Tesla made a
       voluntary decision to recall Model 3 and Y vehicles that may have been
       manufactured or serviced with the component described above with a supplier
       manufacture date of June 2, 2023, or later.

   •   On April 8, 2024, Tesla petitioned for exemption from the Safety Act’s notiﬁcation
       and remedy requirements on the basis that this noncompliance is inconsequential
       as it relates to motor vehicle safety. Tesla amended its petition on May 3, 2024.

   •   On July 17, 2026, NHTSA denied Tesla’s petition.

   •   As of August 2, 2026, Tesla has identiﬁed 3 warranty claims and 1 ﬁeld report for
       U.S. vehicles that are related to or may be related to this condition. Tesla is not
       aware of any crashes, injuries, or deaths related to this condition.
•   On July 24, 2026, Tesla timely amended its Part 573 Noncompliance Recall Report
    related to Recall 24V-208 to update the safety risk, chronology, remedy, and recall
    schedule sections.

•   On July 28, 2026, NHTSA rejected Tesla’s amended Part 573 Noncompliance Recall
    Report and requested that Tesla ﬁle a new Part 573 Noncompliance Recall Report
    following denial of Tesla’s inconsequential petition.

•   On July 28, 2026, Tesla met with NHTSA’s Recall Management Division to
    understand the request for a new Part 573 report and on July 29 and August 3, 2026,
    Tesla met with NHTSA’s Office of Chief Counsel to understand the request for a new
    Part 573 report.

•   Per NHTSA’s request, Tesla is ﬁling a new Part 573 Noncompliance Recall Report.
    Tesla notes this Part 573 Report covers the same noncompliance described in
    Recall 24V-208, originally ﬁled on March 15, 2024.

•   Based on a query of service data on July 30, 2026, Tesla discovered that the
    noncompliant lamps inadvertently continued to be consumed in service until May 3,
    2024. As of May 3, 2024, the noncompliant parts are no longer being consumed in
    service. Due to this error, 432 vehicles are being added to the affected vehicle list for
    this recall.

Defect / Noncompliance Description

Description of the defect or noncompliance:
On affected vehicles, the headlamp low-beam output exceeds the maximum allowed intensity in the
outer upper-right and outer upper-left areas of the 10U to 90U zone, as prescribed in FMVSS No. 108.

FMVSS1: 108 - Lamps, reflective devices, and assoc. Equipment
FMVSS2:
Description of the safety risk, including crash, fire, death, injury:
Headlamp low-beam output that exceeds the maximum allowed intensity may reduce visibility for the
driver and other road users, increasing the risk of crash.

Description of remedy program:
At no cost to customers, Tesla will replace the headlamps on affected vehicles with headlamps that are

The remedy component is compliant with FMVSS No. 108 whereas the recalled component exceeds the
FMVSS No. 108 maximum allowed intensity for low-beam output.

                                        IMPORTANT SAFETY RECALL
<Date>

<Name>
<Address>
<Address 2> (if applicable; if not, remove this line)
<City, State, ZIP>

This notice applies to your vehicle, <VIN>

NHTSA Recall No: 26V-507

Dear Tesla Owner,

This notice is sent to you in accordance with the National Traffic and Motor Vehicle Safety Act. Tesla, Inc. has
decided that certain Model Year 2017 – 2023 Model 3 and Model Year 2020 – 2023 Model Y vehicles fail to conform
to Federal Motor Vehicle Safety Standard No. 108, “Lamps, Reflective Devices, and Associated Equipment.” Our
records indicate that you are the owner of a vehicle affected by this action.

REASON FOR THIS RECALL

The headlamp low-beam output exceeds the maximum allowed intensity, as prescribed
in FMVSS No. 108. Headlamp low-beam output that exceeds the maximum allowed intensity may reduce visibility for
the driver and other road users, increasing the risk of a crash.

WHAT TESLA WILL DO

At no charge to the customer, Tesla will remove and replace both the left-hand and right-hand headlamps with new
headlamps. The repair is estimated to take 30-45 minutes to complete.

WHAT YOU SHOULD DO

Replacement headlamp parts are currently unavailable. We are working closely with our suppliers and supply chain
teams to source the required parts for the recall remedy repair. Once parts are available, we will notify you to
schedule a service appointment through your Tesla mobile App.

If you have already had this repair completed on your vehicle, no further action is required. To confirm whether the
repair was completed on your vehicle, use our online VIN look-up tool at https://www.Tesla.com/Vin-Recall-Search.

Federal law requires any lessor who receives a notification of a determination of a noncompliance pertaining to any
leased motor vehicle to send the notice to the lessee within 10 days.

If you no longer own the vehicle, update vehicle ownership in your Tesla Account. For more details on adding or
removing vehicles from your Tesla Account, visit www.tesla.com/support/account-support#add-remove-products.
